corresponding to a third group of terms, and,

\displaystyle\big(1+\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even}\text{ }i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even}\text{ }i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to a fifth group of terms.

Altogether,

\displaystyle B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\equiv\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)\text{ }\sigma^{-}_{N-3}\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\bigg[\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-}{\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,-}{\underset{i\equiv 2,+}{\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+}{\underset{i\equiv 1,-}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 1,-}{\underset{\mathrm{odd}\text{ }i\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-}_{N-2}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,\text{ }\mathrm{odd}\text{ }i\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\sigma^{-}_{N}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(1+\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even}\text{ }i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{{\underset{\mathrm{even}\text{ }i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-}{\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+\sigma^{-}_{N-2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{\mathrm{even}\text{ }i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 1,-}{\underset{\mathrm{odd}\text{ }i\text{ }:1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,2,-}{\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,-\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,1,+\eta}{\underset{0\leq i\leq 1,i\equiv 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,2,-}{\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,-\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,1+\eta}{\underset{0\leq i\leq 1,i\equiv 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\sigma^{-}_{N-2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{\mathrm{even}\text{ }i\text{ }i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-}{\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }

\displaystyle+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+}{\underset{i\equiv 1,-}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)

\displaystyle+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-}{\underset{\mathrm{even}\text{ }i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

from which we conclude the argument.

Lemma

3
(
collecting terms from the product of two by two L-operators
). The third entry of the
L
operator product,

\displaystyle\overset{3}{\underset{i=0}{\prod}}L_{\alpha,N-i}\big(\lambda_{\alpha},v_{N-i}\big)\text{, }

has an expansion of the form,

\displaystyle C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\equiv\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-n}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-n}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\sigma^{+}_{N-i}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-i}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-i}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 1,-}{\underset{\mathrm{odd\text{ }}i\text{ }:1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\text{ }

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+\sigma^{+}_{N-(n-3)}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\bigg[\sigma^{+}_{N}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-n}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-n}\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\sigma^{+}_{N-1}\bigg]\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

while the third entry of the L-operator product,

\displaystyle\prod_{i=0}^{2}L_{\alpha,N-i}\big(\lambda_{\alpha},v_{N-i}\big)\text{, }

has an expansion of the form,

\displaystyle C_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\equiv\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\big)\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)\bigg[\underset{i\equiv 2,+\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-\eta}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\big(\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\big)^{2}\sigma^{+}_{N-i-1}+\big(\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\big)\bigg[\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

Proof of Lemma 3
. We collect terms from the first two terms in the product of
L
operators,

\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)&\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N}\\
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)&\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\\
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-1}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

from which one obtains a resultant matrix of the form,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}&\textbf{2}\\
\textbf{3}&\textbf{4}\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

which has the following expression for the third, and fourth, entries, with,

\displaystyle\textbf{3}^{0}\equiv\textbf{3}\equiv\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-1}\text{, }

\displaystyle\textbf{4}^{0}\equiv\textbf{4}\equiv\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\text{, }

From the product of operators,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}&\textbf{2}\\
\textbf{3}&\textbf{4}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)&\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-2}\\
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\end{bmatrix}\cdots\times\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{1}\big)&\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{1}\\
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{1}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{1}\big)\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

which takes the form,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)+\textbf{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}&\textbf{1}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-2}+\textbf{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\\
\textbf{3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)+\textbf{4}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}&\textbf{3}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-2}+\textbf{4}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\end{bmatrix}

\cdots\times\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{1}\big)&\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{1}\\
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{1}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{1}\big)\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

performing rearrangements for the next term yields,

\displaystyle\textbf{3}^{1}\equiv\textbf{3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)+\textbf{4}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}

\displaystyle\equiv\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-1}\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)

\displaystyle+\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\text{, }

\displaystyle\equiv\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-1}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)+\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\text{, }

corresponding to the third term,
\textbf{3}^{1}
which has the product representation,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\big)\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)\underset{i\equiv 2,+\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-\eta}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}

\displaystyle\times\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\big(\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\big)^{2}\sigma^{+}_{N-i-1}+\big(\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\big)\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\text{. }

The fourth term,
\textbf{4}^{1}
, has the product representation,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\bigg]+\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\text{. }

From the two by two matrix with entries
\textbf{1}^{1}
,
\textbf{2}^{1}
,
\textbf{3}^{1}
and
\textbf{4}^{1}
, the remaining terms of the product takes the form,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{1}&\textbf{2}^{1}\\

\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{1}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{1}\big)\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

which implies, for,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{1}&\textbf{2}^{1}\\
\textbf{3}^{1}&\textbf{4}^{1}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)&\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-3}\\
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-3}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)\end{bmatrix}\equiv\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{2}&\textbf{2}^{2}\\
\textbf{3}^{2}&\textbf{4}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

has a third entry that is given by,

\displaystyle\textbf{3}^{2}\equiv\textbf{3}^{1}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)+\textbf{4}^{1}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-3}\text{. }

The superposition above has the equivalent product representation, after collecting terms,

\displaystyle\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\big)\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)\underset{i\equiv 2,+\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-\eta}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}

\displaystyle\times\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\big(\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\big)^{2}\sigma^{+}_{N-i-1}+\big(\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\big)\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)+\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-i}\big)\bigg]\sigma^{+}_{N-3}\text{. }

Distributing terms further implies,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\big)\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)\text{ }\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)

\displaystyle\times\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }{\underset{i\equiv 0,-\eta}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\text{ }\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\big(\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\big)^{2}\sigma^{+}_{N-i-1}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\sigma^{+}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\text{ }\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\text{ }\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\text{ }\sigma^{+}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-}{\underset{\mathrm{odd\text{ }}i\text{ }:1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\sigma^{+}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

From the superposition above, grouping together terms based upon the occurrence of a
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)
prefactor yields,

\displaystyle\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\bigg[\sigma^{+}_{N}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-\eta}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)

\displaystyle\times\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

while grouping together terms based upon the occurrence of a
\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}
prefactor yields,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\sigma^{+}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\text{ }\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\text{ }\sigma^{+}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-}{\underset{\mathrm{odd\text{ }}i\text{ }:1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+\sigma^{+}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

Besides the
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)
and
\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}
terms, the remaining term under
\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}
is,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

Next, we further rearrange terms under the
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)
, in which,

\displaystyle\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\bigg[\sigma^{+}_{N}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-\eta}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

which yields,

\displaystyle\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\bigg[\sigma^{+}_{N}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-n}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-n}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sigma^{+}_{N-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

Similarly, the terms under the
\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}
yield,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\sigma^{+}_{N-i}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-i}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-i}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 1,-}{\underset{\mathrm{odd\text{ }}i\text{ }:1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+\sigma^{+}_{N-(n-3)}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

while rearranging terms under the
\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}
prefactor yields,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-n}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-n}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

Hence,

\displaystyle C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\equiv\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-n}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-n}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\sigma^{+}_{N-i}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-i}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 1,-}{\underset{\mathrm{odd\text{ }}i\text{ }:1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,+}{\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+\sigma^{+}_{N-(n-3)}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\bigg[\sigma^{+}_{N}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-n}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-n}\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i\text{ }:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\sigma^{+}_{N-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

from which we conclude the argument.

Lemma

4
(
collecting terms from the product of two by two L-operators
). The fourth entry of the
L
operator product,

\displaystyle\overset{3}{\underset{i=0}{\prod}}L_{\alpha,N-i}\big(\lambda_{\alpha},v_{N-i}\big)\text{, }

has an expansion of the form,

\displaystyle D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\equiv\underset{0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{\mathrm{odd}\text{ }i\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 0,i\equiv 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\big(\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\big)^{2}\bigg]\sigma^{+}_{N-i-1}+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\sigma^{+}_{N}\sigma^{+}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-\eta}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\sigma^{-}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{,
}

while the fourth entry of the L-operator product,

\displaystyle\prod_{i=0}^{2}L_{\alpha,N-i}\big(\lambda_{\alpha},v_{N-i}\big)\text{, }

has an expansion of the form,

\displaystyle D_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\equiv\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\bigg]\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\bigg]+\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\text{. }

Proof of Lemma 4
. We collect terms from the first two terms in the product of
L
operators,

\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)&\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N}\\
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)&\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\\
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-1}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

from which one obtains a resultant matrix of the form,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}&\textbf{2}\\
\textbf{3}&\textbf{4}\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

which has the following expression for the third and fourth entries, with,

\displaystyle\textbf{3}^{0}\equiv\textbf{3}\equiv\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-1}\text{ }\text{ , }

\displaystyle\textbf{4}^{0}\equiv\textbf{4}\equiv\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\text{, }

Performing rearrangements for the final term, yields,

\displaystyle\textbf{4}^{1}\equiv\textbf{3}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-2}+\textbf{4}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)

\displaystyle\equiv\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-1}\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-2}

\displaystyle+\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)

\displaystyle\equiv\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-2}+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-1}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-2}

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-1}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)

\displaystyle+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\text{, }

corresponding to the fourth term,
\textbf{4}^{1}
which has the product representation,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\bigg]\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\bigg]+\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\text{. }

As demonstrated in arguments for the previous lemma, the third term has the product expansion,

\displaystyle\textbf{3}^{1}\equiv\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-\eta}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\big(\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\big)^{2}\bigg]\sigma^{+}_{N-i-1}+\big(\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

From the two by two matrix with entries
\textbf{1}^{1}
,
\textbf{2}^{1}
,
\textbf{3}^{1}
and
\textbf{4}^{1}
, the remaining terms of the product takes the form,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{1}&\textbf{2}^{1}\\
\textbf{3}^{1}&\textbf{4}^{1}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)&\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-3}\\
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-3}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)\end{bmatrix}\cdots\times\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{1}\big)&\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{1}\\
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{1}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{1}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{1}\big)\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

which implies, for,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{1}&\textbf{2}^{1}\\
\textbf{3}^{1}&\textbf{4}^{1}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)&\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-3}\\
\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-3}&\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)\end{bmatrix}\equiv\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{2}&\textbf{2}^{2}\\
\textbf{3}^{2}&\textbf{4}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

has a fourth entry that is given by,

\displaystyle\textbf{4}^{2}\equiv\textbf{3}^{1}\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-3}+\textbf{4}^{1}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)\text{. }

The superposition above has the equivalent product representation, after substituting terms,

\displaystyle\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-\eta}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\big(\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\big)^{2}\bigg]\sigma^{+}_{N-i-1}+\big(\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{N-3}

\displaystyle+\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-1}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-3}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-3}\big)\text{. }

Proceeding further from the superposition above, we group terms under the
\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}
prefactor, from which,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\sigma^{+}_{N}\sigma^{+}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\sigma^{-}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-\eta}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\sigma^{-}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

while the remaining term, for the first group of terms, under the
\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}
prefactor, is,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\big(\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\big)^{2}\bigg]\sigma^{+}_{N-i-1}\text{. }

In the second group of terms, collecting terms under the prefactor
\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}
yields,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{\mathrm{odd}\text{ }i\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 0,i\equiv 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

while for the remaining term,

\displaystyle\underset{0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-i}\big)\text{. }

The desired expression for the final entry of the monodromy matrix,
D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
, takes the form,

\displaystyle D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\equiv\underset{0\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 0,+}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 3,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{\mathrm{odd}\text{ }i\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,-}{\underset{i\equiv 1,+}{\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 0,i\equiv 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-2}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 1}{\prod}\big(\sigma^{-}_{N-i}\big)^{2}\bigg]\sigma^{+}_{N-i-1}+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\sigma^{+}_{N}\sigma^{+}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sigma^{-}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\equiv 2,+\eta}{\underset{i\equiv 0,-\eta}{\underset{\mathrm{even\text{ }}i:\text{ }0\leq i\leq 2}{\prod}}}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]+\sigma^{+}_{N-2}\sigma^{-}_{N-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{0\leq i\leq 1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{N-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{N-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{,
}

from which we conclude the argument.

With expressions for
A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
,
B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
,
C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
and
D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
, below we obtain other expressions for,

\begin{bmatrix}A_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)&B_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\\
C_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)&D_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

in terms of,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{n}&\textbf{2}^{n}\\
\textbf{3}^{n}&\textbf{4}^{n}\end{bmatrix}\text{. }

Lemma

4
(
further iterating the matrix computation
). The
n
th L-operator,

\begin{bmatrix}A_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)&B_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\\
C_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)&D_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\end{bmatrix}\equiv\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{n}&\textbf{2}^{n}\\
\textbf{3}^{n}&\textbf{4}^{n}\end{bmatrix}\equiv\prod_{1\leq i\leq n}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{i}&\textbf{2}^{i}\\
\textbf{3}^{i}&\textbf{4}^{i}\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

can be expressed in terms of,

\begin{bmatrix}\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}_{1}+\mathscr{A}_{2}+\mathscr{A}_{3}\bigg]&\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{B}_{1}+\mathscr{B}_{2}+\mathscr{B}_{3}\bigg]\\
\bigg[C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{C}_{1}+\mathscr{C}_{2}+\mathscr{C}_{3}\bigg]&\bigg[C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{D}_{1}+\mathscr{D}_{2}+\mathscr{D}_{3}\bigg]\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

for,

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{1}\equiv\mathscr{C}_{1}\equiv\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{i}\equiv\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\text{, }

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{2}\equiv\mathscr{C}_{2}\equiv\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{A}_{2}\big)_{i}\equiv\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}\equiv\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\text{, }

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{3}\equiv\mathscr{C}_{3}\equiv\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{3}\big)_{i,j}\bigg]\equiv\text{ }\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the first and third entries,

\displaystyle\mathscr{B}_{1}\equiv\mathscr{D}_{1}\equiv\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{D}_{1}\big)_{i}\equiv\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\text{, }

\displaystyle\mathscr{B}_{2}\equiv\mathscr{D}_{2}\equiv\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{B}_{2}\big)_{i}\equiv\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{D}_{2}\big)_{i}\equiv\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\text{, }

\displaystyle\mathscr{B}_{3}\equiv\mathscr{D}_{3}\equiv\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{2\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{2\leq i\leq m}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{D}_{3}\big)_{i,j}\bigg]\equiv\text{ }\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the second and fourth entries.

Proof of Lemma 4
. By direct computation, we begin by computing the product of L-operators,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{0}&\textbf{2}^{0}\\
\textbf{3}^{0}&\textbf{4}^{0}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{1}&\textbf{2}^{1}\\
\textbf{3}^{1}&\textbf{4}^{1}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{2}&\textbf{2}^{2}\\
\textbf{3}^{2}&\textbf{4}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{3}&\textbf{2}^{3}\\
\textbf{3}^{3}&\textbf{4}^{3}\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

after which we compute the product of three arbitrary L-operators,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{i-3}&\textbf{2}^{i-3}\\
\textbf{3}^{i-3}&\textbf{4}^{i-3}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{i-2}&\textbf{2}^{i-2}\\
\textbf{3}^{i-2}&\textbf{4}^{i-2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{i-1}&\textbf{2}^{i-1}\\
\textbf{3}^{i-1}&\textbf{4}^{i-1}\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

from the
n
fold product,

\prod_{1\leq i\leq n}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{i}&\textbf{2}^{i}\\
\textbf{3}^{i}&\textbf{4}^{i}\end{bmatrix}\text{. }

For the first product of two by two L-operators, for
i\equiv 0
,
i\equiv 1
,
i\equiv 2
, and
i\equiv 3
, by brute force one has,

\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{1}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{3}^{1}&\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{2}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\\
\textbf{1}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{3}^{1}\textbf{4}^{0}&\textbf{2}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{4}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{2}&\textbf{2}^{2}\\
\textbf{3}^{2}&\textbf{4}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{3}&\textbf{2}^{3}\\
\textbf{3}^{3}&\textbf{4}^{3}\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

is equivalent to,

\begin{bmatrix}\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{1}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{3}^{1}\big)\textbf{1}^{2}+\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{2}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\textbf{3}^{2}&\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{1}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{3}^{1}\big)\textbf{2}^{2}+\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{2}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\textbf{4}^{2}\\
\big(\textbf{1}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{3}^{1}\textbf{4}^{0}\big)\textbf{1}^{2}+\big(\textbf{2}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{4}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\textbf{3}^{2}&\big(\textbf{1}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{3}^{1}\textbf{4}^{0}\big)\textbf{2}^{2}+\big(\textbf{2}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{4}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\textbf{4}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{3}&\textbf{2}^{3}\\
\textbf{3}^{3}&\textbf{4}^{3}\end{bmatrix}\text{. }

From previous arguments given in
Lemmas

1
-
4
, the fact that,

\begin{bmatrix}A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)&B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\\
C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)&D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

equals,

\begin{bmatrix}\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{1}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{3}^{1}\big)\textbf{1}^{2}+\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{2}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\textbf{3}^{2}&\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{1}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{3}^{1}\big)\textbf{2}^{2}+\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{2}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\textbf{4}^{2}\\
\big(\textbf{1}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{3}^{1}\textbf{4}^{0}\big)\textbf{1}^{2}+\big(\textbf{2}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{4}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\textbf{3}^{2}&\big(\textbf{1}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{3}^{1}\textbf{4}^{0}\big)\textbf{2}^{2}+\big(\textbf{2}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{4}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\textbf{4}^{2}\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

implies, entry by entry, that,

\displaystyle A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\equiv\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{1}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{3}^{1}\big)\textbf{1}^{2}+\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{2}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\textbf{3}^{2}\text{, }

\displaystyle B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\equiv\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{1}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{3}^{1}\big)\textbf{2}^{2}+\big(\textbf{1}^{0}\textbf{2}^{1}+\textbf{2}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\text{, }

\displaystyle C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\equiv\big(\textbf{1}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{3}^{1}\textbf{4}^{0}\big)\textbf{1}^{2}+\big(\textbf{2}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{4}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\textbf{3}^{2}\text{, }

\displaystyle D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\equiv\big(\textbf{1}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{3}^{1}\textbf{4}^{0}\big)\textbf{2}^{2}+\big(\textbf{2}^{1}\textbf{3}^{0}+\textbf{4}^{0}\textbf{4}^{1}\big)\textbf{4}^{2}\text{. }

In terms of
A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
,
B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
,
C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
and
D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
, performing the final multiplication of L-operators,

\begin{bmatrix}A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)&B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\\
C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)&D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{3}&\textbf{2}^{3}\\
\textbf{3}^{3}&\textbf{4}^{3}\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

equals,

\begin{bmatrix}A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{1}^{3}+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{3}^{3}&A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{2}^{3}+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{4}^{3}\\
C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{1}^{3}+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{3}^{3}&C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{2}^{3}+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{4}^{3}\end{bmatrix}\text{. }

Hence,

\prod_{0\leq i\leq 3}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{i}&\textbf{2}^{i}\\
\textbf{3}^{i}&\textbf{4}^{i}\end{bmatrix}\equiv\begin{bmatrix}A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{1}^{3}+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{3}^{3}&A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{2}^{3}+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{4}^{3}\\
C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{1}^{3}+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{3}^{3}&C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{2}^{3}+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{4}^{3}\end{bmatrix}\text{. }

With such an expression, we make use of the following relations,

\displaystyle A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=A_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{1}^{2}+B_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{3}^{2}\equiv A_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{2}\big)+B_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{2}\text{, }

\displaystyle B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=A_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{2}^{2}+B_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{4}^{2}\equiv A_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{2}+B_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{2}\big)\text{, }

\displaystyle C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=C_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{1}^{2}+D_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{4}^{2}\equiv C_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{2}\big)+D_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{2}\text{, }

\displaystyle D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=C_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{2}^{2}+D_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{4}^{2}\equiv C_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{2}+D_{2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{2}\big)\text{. }

To finish the arguments for
Lemma

4
, we introduce the result below.

Lemma

5
(
iteratively obtaining the entries of the n th L-operator from the entries of the third L-operator
). The first entry of the
n
th L-operator can be expressed in terms of,

\displaystyle A_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

The second entry of the
n
th L-operator can be expressed in terms of,

\displaystyle B_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-4}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

The third entry of the
n
th L-operator can be expressed in terms of,

\displaystyle C_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=\bigg[C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

The fourth entry of the
n
th L-operator can be expressed in terms of,

\displaystyle D_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=\bigg[C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-4}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

Proof of Lemma 5
. Observe, for the first entry of the
n
th L-operator,

\prod_{1\leq i\leq n}\begin{bmatrix}\textbf{1}^{i}&\textbf{2}^{i}\\
\textbf{3}^{i}&\textbf{4}^{i}\end{bmatrix}\text{, }

that,

\displaystyle A_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=A_{n-1}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{1}^{n-1}+B_{n-1}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{3}^{n-1}\equiv A_{n-1}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)+B_{n-1}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-1}\text{. }

(1)

From the equality above, making the substitution for
A_{n-1}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
in terms of
A_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
, implies,

\displaystyle A_{n-1}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=A_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{1}^{n-2}+B_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{3}^{n-2}\equiv A_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)+B_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-2}\text{. }

Similarly,

\displaystyle B_{n-1}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=A_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{2}^{n-2}+B_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\textbf{4}^{n-2}\equiv A_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-2}+B_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)\text{. }

(
1^{*}
)

Rewriting
(1)
with the expression for
A_{n-1}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
and
B_{n-1}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
implies,

\displaystyle(1)\equiv\bigg[A_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)+B_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-2}\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-2}+B_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-1}\text{. }

Grouping together like terms from the expression above,

\displaystyle A_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+B_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-1}\bigg]\text{. }

(2)

Continuing along similar lines, in which we substitute for
A_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
and
B_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
to rewrite (2) implies,

\displaystyle(2)\equiv\bigg[A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-3}\big)+B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-3}\bigg]\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-3}+B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-3}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-3}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)+\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-1}\bigg]\text{, }

from which performing additional rearrangements implies, for the first term, that,

\displaystyle A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]+B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-3}\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-3}\big)\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]+B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 3}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\text{, }

(
2^{*}
)

and for the second term, that,

\displaystyle A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)\bigg[\prod_{2\leq i\leq 3}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-2}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\text{ }\mathrm{odd}\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]+A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\text{ }\mathrm{odd}\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

(
2^{**}
)

To extrapolate the formula to
A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
, from the previous two terms above, substitute for
A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
and
B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
, in which, from
(2^{*})
,

\displaystyle A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

equals,

\displaystyle\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-4}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\prod_{1\leq i\leq 3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

which is equivalent to,

\displaystyle A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\prod_{1\leq i\leq 4}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-4}\bigg[\text{ }\prod_{1\leq i\leq 3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

(
2^{*}-1
)

From
(2^{*})
, the second term with
A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
,

\displaystyle A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-3}\big)\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\text{, }

equals,

\displaystyle\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-4}\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-3}\big)\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\text{, }

which is equivalent to,

\displaystyle A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\prod_{3\leq i\leq 4}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\bigg[\text{ }\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-3}\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-4}\text{. }

(
2^{*}-2
)

From
(2^{*})
, the third entry with
B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
,

\displaystyle B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-3}\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

equals,

\displaystyle\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-4}+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-3}\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

which is equivalent to,

\displaystyle A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\prod_{3\leq i\leq 4}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-3}

\displaystyle\times\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

(
2^{*}-3
)

From
(2^{*})
, the fourth entry with
B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
,

\displaystyle B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 3}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\text{, }

equals,

\displaystyle\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-4}+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 3}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\text{, }

which is equivalent to,

\displaystyle A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{4}\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 4}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\bigg[\prod_{1\leq i\leq 3}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\text{. }

(
2^{*}-4
)

Continuing along similar lines for each term in
(2^{**})
implies, that the first term,

\displaystyle A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)\bigg[\prod_{2\leq i\leq 3}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{, }

equals,

\displaystyle\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-4}\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)\bigg[\prod_{2\leq i\leq 3}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{, }

which is equivalent to,

\displaystyle A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\bigg[\prod_{2\leq i\leq 3}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}

\displaystyle\times\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)\bigg[\prod_{2\leq i\leq 4}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{. }

(
2^{**}-1
)

From
(2^{**})
, the second term,

\displaystyle B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\text{ }\mathrm{odd}\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

equals,

\displaystyle\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-4}+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\text{ }\mathrm{odd}\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

which is equivalent to,

\displaystyle A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\sigma^{+}_{n-2}\sigma^{-}_{n-4}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\text{ }\mathrm{odd}\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)

\displaystyle\times\sigma^{+}_{n-2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\text{ }\mathrm{odd}\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

(
2^{**}-2
)

From
(2^{**})
, the third term,

\displaystyle A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\text{ }\mathrm{odd}\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{, }

equals,

\displaystyle\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-4}\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\text{ }\mathrm{odd}\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]\text{, }

which is equivalent to,

\displaystyle A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\text{ }\mathrm{odd}\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-4}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{i\text{ }\mathrm{odd}\text{ }:\text{ }1\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{N-j}\bigg]\text{. }

(
2^{**}-3
)

From
(2^{**})
, the fourth term,

\displaystyle B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

equals,

\displaystyle\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-4}+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

is equivalent to,

\displaystyle A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\sigma^{+}_{n-1}\sigma^{+}_{n-4}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\text{ }\bigg]+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-1}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 4}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

(
2^{**}-4
)

Extrapolating the formulas from
(1)
and
(2^{*})
yields,

\displaystyle A_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

Along similar lines, repeating the computations since the beginning of the proof yields another formula for
C_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
, in which,

\displaystyle C_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=\bigg[C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[C_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+D_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

To obtain the desired formula for
B_{n-1}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
, we pursue the following steps. Rewriting
(1^{*})
, in terms of
A_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
and
B_{n-2}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
, implies,

\displaystyle(1^{*})\equiv\bigg[A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-3}\big)+B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-3}\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-2}

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-3}+B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-3}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-3}\big)\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)\text{, }

which is equivalent to the superposition,

\displaystyle A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-3}\big)+B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\sigma^{+,-}_{n-i}\bigg]\text{, }

for the first term, and,

\displaystyle A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)+B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

for the second term.

From the two terms above, substituting for
A_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
and
B_{n-3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
yields, for the first term,

\displaystyle\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-4}\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-3}\big)

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-4}+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\sigma^{+,-}_{n-i}\bigg]\text{, }

(
1^{*}-1
)

while for the second term,

\displaystyle\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-4}+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-4}\bigg]\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-3}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-4}+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

(
1^{*}-2
)

Performing rearrangements from
(1^{*}-1)
implies,

\displaystyle A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-2}\bigg[\underset{3\leq i\leq 4}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\sigma^{-}_{n-2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-3}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-3}\big)\sigma^{+}_{n-4}

\displaystyle+A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 4}{\prod}\sigma^{+,-}_{n-i}\bigg]+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-4}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-4}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\sigma^{+,-}_{n-i}\bigg]\text{, }

while performing rearrangements from
(1^{*}-2)
of terms implies,

\displaystyle A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{3\leq i\leq 4}{\prod}\sigma^{-}_{n-i}\bigg]+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{2}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-2}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-2}\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{3\leq i\leq 4}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+A_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\sigma^{-}_{n-4}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]+B_{n-4}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq 4}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}-\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

Extrapolating the formulas from previous computations yields,

\displaystyle B_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)=\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-4}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[A_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)+B_{3}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{2\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

Along similar lines, repeating the computations since the beginning of the proof yields another formula for
D_{n}\big(\lambda_{\alpha}\big)
, in which,
