\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{3}\approx\bigg[{\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial\big[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}\big]}{\partial u}\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}+\frac{\partial\big[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}\big]}{\partial u}\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[{\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial\big[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}\big]}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}+\frac{\partial\big[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}\big]}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{3}\big)_{i,j}\bigg]\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\frac{B_{3}\big(u\big)A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{u-u^{\prime}}+\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\frac{A_{3}\big(u\big)B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{u-u^{\prime}}\bigg]\text{. }

Proof of Lemma 8
. The third term,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{2}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{3}\bigg\}\text{, }

is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}

\displaystyle\times\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg\}\text{. }

Applying Leibniz’ rule to the Poisson bracket over
\mathscr{P}
yields the expression,

\displaystyle\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}

\displaystyle+\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\{\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3},\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

which, by anticommutativity of the Poisson bracket, equals,

\displaystyle-\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg],\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg\}\text{. }

Applying Leibniz’ rule again to each Poisson bracket in the expression above yields,

\displaystyle-\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\big\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\bigg[\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the first term, and,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg[\big\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\big\}\bigg[\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg],\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg\}\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the second term. In the first term to which we applied the Poisson bracket, the fact that,

\displaystyle\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

implies that another application of Leibniz’ rule yields,

\displaystyle-\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\big\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\bigg[\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg\{\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1},\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg\{\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

Applying Leibniz’ rule to the final Poisson bracket in the superposition above yields,

\displaystyle-\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\big\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\bigg[\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\bigg\{\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1},\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\text{ }\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

From the expression above, writing each Poisson bracket individually yields, as summations over
\mathscr{P}
,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\big\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}=-\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\big\{\mathscr{P}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{2}\big\}=-\bigg[\big\{B_{3}\big(u\big),A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}+\big\{A_{3}\big(u\big),B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}\bigg]\text{, }

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1},\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\equiv 0\text{, }

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\text{, }

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\text{ }\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\equiv 0\text{. }

Evaluating the third Poisson bracket is related to the computation of the derivative,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

can be differentiated term by term, with respect to
u^{\prime}
, to obtain,

\displaystyle\mathrm{cos}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-1}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)+\cdots+\mathrm{cos}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-1}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)+\cdots+\bigg[\mathrm{cos}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-1}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\bigg[\underset{2\leq j\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-1}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)\bigg]+\cdots

\displaystyle+\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq m-1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\mathrm{cos}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-m}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-m}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

which we denote as
\mathscr{D}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\equiv\mathscr{D}
. This implies the following expression,

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}\frac{\partial\mathscr{P}_{1}}{\partial u^{\prime}}+\frac{\partial\mathscr{P}_{1}}{\partial u}\mathscr{D}^{\prime}\text{, }

corresponding to the third bracket, for
\mathscr{D}^{\prime}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\equiv\mathscr{D}^{\prime}
, which equals,

\displaystyle\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}+\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}\text{, }

while for the fourth bracket, a similar equality,

\displaystyle\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}+\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}\text{. }

holds. Altogether, putting each of the computations together provides the desired estimate, from which we conclude the argument.

2.4.4
Fourth Poisson bracket,
\mathcal{P}_{4}

Lemma

9
(
evaluating the fourth Poisson bracket in the first relation
). The fourth term,
\mathcal{P}_{4}
, approximately equals,

\displaystyle-\big[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{i}\big]\bigg[\frac{A_{3}\big(u\big)B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{u-u^{\prime}}+\frac{B_{3}\big(u\big)A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{u-u^{\prime}}\bigg]+\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\mathscr{A}_{2}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}

\displaystyle+B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]+\bigg[A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}+B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

Proof of Lemma 9
. The fourth term,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1}\mathscr{A}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg\}\text{, }

is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg\}\text{. }

One application of Leibniz’ rule to the Poisson bracket above gives,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg[\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg\}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg]\text{, }

while a second application of Liebniz’ rule to the first Poisson bracket above gives,

\displaystyle-\big\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}-\bigg\{\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{2}\text{, }

(*)

after anticommuting terms in the first Poisson bracket,

\displaystyle\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg\}\text{, }

with,

\displaystyle\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg\}=-\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\text{. }

Applying Leibniz’ rule to the second Poisson bracket in (*) gives,

\displaystyle\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\big\{\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}+\big\{\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg]\text{. }

For the second Poisson bracket,

\displaystyle\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{1}\text{, }

anticommuting terms gives,

\displaystyle-\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{1}\text{, }

to which we apply Leibniz’ rule,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg[\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}+\bigg\{\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}

\displaystyle\times\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg]\text{. }

Observe that the second Poisson bracket above is equal to,

\displaystyle\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg\{\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{. }

Similarly, from (*), from the second Poisson bracket,

\displaystyle\bigg\{\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{2}\text{, }

to which we apply Leibniz’ rule implies,

\displaystyle\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\big\{\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}+\big\{\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg]\text{. }

Hence, the Poisson bracket that was rearranged corresponding to the fourth term is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg[-\big\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}+\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\big\{\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}+\big\{\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\mathscr{P}_{1}

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{1}\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg\{\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\bigg]\text{. }

As a summation over
\mathscr{P}
, writing out each Poisson bracket from the superposition above gives,

\displaystyle-\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\big\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}=-\bigg[\big\{A_{3}\big(u\big),B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}+\big\{B_{3}\big(u\big),A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\text{ }\text{ , }

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\big\{\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}+\big\{\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg]\equiv 0\text{ }\text{ , }

\displaystyle-\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\mathscr{P}_{1}\text{ }\text{ , }

\displaystyle-\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\mathscr{P}_{1}\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg\{\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

where in the second Poisson bracket, we made use of the fact that,

\displaystyle\big\{\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},B_{3}\big(u\big)\big\}=0\text{, }

\displaystyle\big\{\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},A_{3}\big(u\big)\big\}=0\text{, }

\displaystyle\big\{\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}=0\text{, }

\displaystyle\text{ }\big\{\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1},B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}=0\text{, }

For the third Poisson bracket above, each term for all possible
\mathscr{P}_{2}
is,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\big(B_{3}\big(u\big)\big)\bigg\{B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\big(B_{3}\big(u\big)\big)\bigg\{A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\big(A_{3}\big(u\big)\big)\bigg\{B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\big(A_{3}\big(u\big)\big)\bigg\{A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

and, for the fourth Poisson bracket,

\displaystyle\bigg\{\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}=0\text{. }

Evaluating each bracket from the five listed above implies,

\displaystyle B_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\text{ }\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the Poisson bracket between
B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)
and the product of sine functions,

\displaystyle B_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\text{ }\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the Poisson bracket between
A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)
and the product of sine functions,

\displaystyle A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\text{ }\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the Poisson bracket between
B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)
and the product of sine functions, and,

\displaystyle A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\text{ }\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg(\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the Poisson bracket between
A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)
and the product of sine functions. From each of the four brackets above, for differentiation with respect to
u^{\prime}
, and then with respect to
u
, one can write,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

as,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\equiv\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathrm{cos}\big(u-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)

\displaystyle\times\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)+\underset{2\leq i\leq n-4}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)

\displaystyle\times\mathrm{cos}\big(u-v_{n-(n-3)}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-(n-3)}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

can be further rearranged as,

\displaystyle\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

after distributing the derivative of
B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)
to each term in the summation, from,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)+\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\underset{2\leq i\leq n-4}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-(n-3)}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-(n-3)}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

As a result, the remaining terms from the Poisson bracket,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

can be combined under the same summation to form,

\displaystyle\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

The observation that the derivative of the product of sine functions,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

equals,

\displaystyle\mathrm{cos}\big(u-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)\bigg[\underset{2\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\cdots+\bigg[\underset{2\leq i\leq n-4}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\mathrm{cos}\big(u-v_{n-(n-3)}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-(n-3)}\big)\text{. }

comes from the fact that the sum of products above can be expressed as,

\displaystyle\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

For the three remaining Poisson brackets, along similar lines,

\displaystyle\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

for the first Poisson bracket,

\displaystyle A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\text{ }\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

for the second Poisson bracket, and,

\displaystyle\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

for the third Poisson bracket, in which the derivatives of
A_{3}
, or
B_{3}
, can be combined with the summation of the
i
th derivative of the sine functions and the remaining product of sine functions. Hence, the terms from each of the four Poisson brackets are equal to,

\displaystyle B_{3}\big(u\big)\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]+B_{3}\big(u\big)

\displaystyle\times\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+A_{3}\big(u\big)

\displaystyle\times\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle+A_{3}\big(u\big)

\displaystyle\times\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

with prefactor,

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\text{. }

Excluding the prefactor above, the four Poisson brackets can be combined under the single summation, yielding,

\displaystyle\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[B_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]+B_{3}\big(u\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]+A_{3}\big(u\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]+A_{3}\big(u\big)

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]+\bigg[\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

which, from the expressions introduced in
Lemma

4
, can be expressed as,

\displaystyle\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\mathscr{A}_{2}\bigg[B_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}+\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]+B_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}

\displaystyle+\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]+A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}+\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}+\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

Grouping together like terms from the above summation implies,

\displaystyle\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\mathscr{A}_{2}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}+B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]+\bigg[A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}

\displaystyle+B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

Altogether, the terms from all nonzero Poisson brackets,

\displaystyle-\bigg[\big\{A_{3}\big(u\big),B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}+\big\{B_{3}\big(u\big),A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}\bigg]\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]+\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\mathscr{A}_{2}\bigg[A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}

\displaystyle+B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}+B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\text{, }

Furthermore, applying expressions for the Poisson brackets between
A_{3}\big(u\big)
,
B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)
, and for the Poisson bracket between
B_{3}\big(u\big)
,
A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)
, from previous terms in the first relation, approximately yields,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1}\mathscr{A}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{2}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{1}\bigg\}\approx-\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n-3}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-i}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\frac{A_{3}\big(u\big)B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{u-u^{\prime}}+\frac{B_{3}\big(u\big)A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{u-u^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\mathscr{A}_{2}\bigg[A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}+B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}+B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\text{, }

from which we conclude the argument.

2.4.5
Fifth Poisson bracket,
\mathcal{P}_{5}

Lemma

10
(
evaluating the fifth Poisson bracket in the first relation
). The fifth term approximately equals,

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{5}\approx-\frac{B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)A_{3}\big(u\big)B_{3}\big(u\big)\mathscr{C}_{2}}{u^{\prime}-u}\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)A_{3}\big(u\big)A_{3}\big(u\big)\mathscr{C}_{2}}{u^{\prime}-u}\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq k\leq n-3}{\sum}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{B_{3}\big(u\big)A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)B_{3}\big(u\big)\mathscr{C}_{2}}{u-u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq k\leq n-3}{\sum}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{B_{3}\big(u\big)A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)A_{3}\big(u\big)\mathscr{C}_{2}}{u-u^{\prime}}\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq k\leq n-3}{\sum}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq k\leq n-3}{\sum}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle+B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq k\leq n-3}{\sum}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq k\leq n-3}{\sum}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq k\leq n-3}{\sum}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq n-3}{\prod}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]\text{. }

Proof of Lemma 10
. The fifth term,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1}\mathscr{A}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{2}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{2}\bigg\}\text{, }

is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

which can be rearranged by applying Leibniz’ rule to the first argument, in which,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg[\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg]\text{, }

to which a second application of Leibniz’ rule yields, for the second argument,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg[-\big\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle-\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg[\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

after anticommuting the first Poisson bracket,

\displaystyle\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

and the second Poisson bracket,

\displaystyle\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{. }

Writing out each Poisson bracket gives,

\displaystyle-\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\big\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]=-\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\big\{B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),A_{3}\big(u\big)\big\}

\displaystyle+\big\{B_{3}\big(u\big),A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}\bigg]\text{, }

\displaystyle-\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{2}=-\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],B_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg\}\bigg]\text{, }

for the first two terms, while the third term,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}

is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\bigg\{B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle+\bigg\{A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\bigg]\text{. }

The fourth term is,

\displaystyle\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{. }

For the first Poisson bracket, one approximately has,

\displaystyle-\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\frac{B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)A_{3}\big(u\big)}{u^{\prime}-u}+\frac{B_{3}\big(u\big)A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{u-u^{\prime}}\bigg]\text{. }

After taking the summation of each bracket over
\mathscr{P}
, observe, from further rearrangement of the third Poisson bracket,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

is equivalent to the terms,

\displaystyle-B_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\{B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

\displaystyle-A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\{A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

\displaystyle-A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\{B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

\displaystyle-B_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\{A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{. }

Term by term, evaluating the bracket for,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]-\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

with prefactor,

\displaystyle-B_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the first bracket, can be expressed with,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg(\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

from the fact that the derivative of the product of sine functions equals,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]=\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-1}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)\cdots\times\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-(n-3)}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-(n-3)}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle=\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

Evaluating the bracket for,

\displaystyle\bigg\{A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big),\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

with prefactor,

\displaystyle-A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the second bracket, can similarly be expressed with,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

with prefactor,

\displaystyle-A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the third bracket. Along similar lines, the third and fourth brackets can be respectively expressed with,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

and with,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

with prefactors,

\displaystyle-A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

and,

\displaystyle-B_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

Grouping together like terms, from the each bracket, from the four above, can be expressed as, either,

\displaystyle\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]-\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

or as,

\displaystyle\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]-\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{. }

Altogether, putting each of the computations together provides the desired estimate, from which we conclude the argument.

2.4.6
Sixth Poisson bracket,
\mathcal{P}_{6}

Lemma

11
(
evaluating the sixth Poisson bracket in the first relation
). The sixth term,
\mathcal{P}_{6}
, approximately equals,

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{6}\approx\frac{A_{3}\big(u\big)B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{u-u^{\prime}}\bigg[{\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{B_{3}\big(u\big)A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{u-u^{\prime}}\bigg[{\underset{m,n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg]\bigg[\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{i}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-2B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\underset{1\leq k\leq m}{\sum}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq m}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{j}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-2B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\underset{1\leq k\leq m}{\sum}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq m}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{j}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-2A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\underset{1\leq k\leq m}{\sum}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq m}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{j}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-2A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[{\underset{1\leq k\leq m}{\sum}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq m}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{j}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-2B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[{\underset{1\leq k\leq m}{\sum}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq m}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{j}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-2B_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[{\underset{1\leq k\leq m}{\sum}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq m}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{j}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-2A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[{\underset{1\leq k\leq m}{\sum}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq m}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{j}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]

\displaystyle-2A_{3}\big(u\big)\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[{\underset{1\leq k\leq m}{\sum}}\frac{\partial\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{k}}{\partial u}\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\neq j\leq m}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}}\big(\mathscr{C}_{1}\big)_{j}\big(\mathscr{C}_{2}\big)_{j}\bigg]\text{. }

Proof of Lemma 11
. The sixth term,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1}\mathscr{A}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{2}\mathscr{A}^{\prime}_{3}\bigg\}\text{, }

is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

is equivalent to the superposition of terms,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{, }

and,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{1}\text{, }

from an application of Leibniz’ rule. Applying Leibniz’ rule a second time to each bracket in the expression above gives,

\displaystyle-\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\big\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}\text{ }{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }

\displaystyle+\bigg\{{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg]\text{ }

\displaystyle-\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{2},\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg\}\bigg[\text{ }{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

after anticommuting terms in,

\displaystyle\bigg\{\mathscr{P}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\text{ }{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg\}\text{, }

and also after performing the same anticommutation in,

\displaystyle\bigg\{\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq n-3}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}+\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{2}\bigg[\text{ }{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}

\displaystyle\times\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg\}\text{. }

Writing out each bracket individually gives,

\displaystyle\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\big\{\mathscr{P}_{1},\mathscr{P}_{1}\big\}=\big\{A_{3}\big(u\big),B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}+\big\{B_{3}\big(u\big),A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}\text{, }

for the first term, which with the corresponding prefactor, equals,

\displaystyle\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\big\{A_{3}\big(u\big),B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}

\displaystyle+\big\{B_{3}\big(u\big),A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}\bigg]\text{, }

while for the remaining brackets,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{2}\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\text{, }

equals,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{2}\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

Altogether, one has the remaining brackets,

\displaystyle\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\big\{A_{3}\big(u\big),B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}

\displaystyle+\big\{B_{3}\big(u\big),A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{2}\underset{\mathscr{P}}{\sum}\bigg\{{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg],\mathscr{P}_{1}\bigg\}\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

The second Poisson bracket is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\bigg\{{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],B_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg\}+\bigg\{{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg],A_{3}\big(u\big)\bigg\}\text{, }

with prefactor,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{2}\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

The two brackets, from the summation over
\mathscr{P}_{2}
, can each analyzed from the following six derivatives,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\big[B_{3}\big(u\big)\big]\text{, }

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\big[A_{3}\big(u\big)\big]\text{, }

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\big[A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big]\text{, }

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\big[B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big]\text{, }

corresponding to the first term. Observe, for the first two derivatives with respective to
u^{\prime}
, and to
u
, above, that,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]=\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle={\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

from the fact that differentiating the product with respect to
u^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)=\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\cdots\times\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-m}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-m}\big)\text{, }

gives,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\cdots\times\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-m}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-m}\big)\bigg]=\mathrm{cos}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-1}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-1}\big)\cdots\times

\displaystyle\underset{2\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)+\cdots+\underset{1\leq i\leq m-1}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\cdots\times

\displaystyle\mathrm{cos}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-m}\pm\eta^{z}_{n-m}\big)\text{, }

which can be expressed with the summation,

\displaystyle\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\text{ }\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

For the other term,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

one similarly obtains,

\displaystyle\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the second term. Altogether, the Poisson bracket for each term takes the form,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{2}\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[{\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}+\bigg[{\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the first Poisson bracket, and,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{2}\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[{\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}+\bigg[{\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

corresponding to the second Poisson bracket. Further rearranging the derivatives from each of the two brackets above implies, for the first bracket,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{2}\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[{\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}+\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{, }

and also, for the second bracket,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{2}\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\sum}\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[{\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}

\displaystyle+\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

Grouping together like terms from the two brackets above yields,

\displaystyle-\mathscr{P}_{2}\underset{n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\sum}\bigg[\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\bigg[{\underset{m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\sum}}}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}+\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u\big)}{\partial u}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\bigg[\mathrm{sin}\big(u-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-i}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\bigg[{\underset{1\leq j\neq i\leq m}{\prod}}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-j}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\bigg[\frac{\partial A_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}+\frac{\partial B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)}{\partial u^{\prime}}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{. }

For the two remaining Poisson brackets appearing before the second bracket, namely,

\displaystyle\bigg[\underset{n^{\prime}:m+n^{\prime}=n-3}{\underset{1\leq j\leq m}{\sum}}\bigg[\text{ }\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq i\leq m}{\prod}\mathrm{sin}\big(u^{\prime}-v_{n-i}\pm\eta\sigma^{z}_{n-j}\big)\bigg]\text{ }\big(\mathrm{sin}\big(2\eta\big)\big)^{n^{\prime}-1}\bigg[\text{ }\underset{1\leq j\leq n^{\prime}}{\prod}\sigma^{-,+}_{n-j}\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\text{ }\bigg]\big\{A_{3}\big(u\big),B_{3}\big(u^{\prime}\big)\big\}\text{, }

and,
